<a id="carpeta-css"></a>
## CARPETA: `css`

<a id="archivo-css-auth-legal-css"></a>
### Archivo: `auth-legal.css`

**RUTA:** `css/auth-legal.css`

**INICIO DEL CONTENIDO: `auth-legal.css`**

```css
.login-body,
.legal-body {
  min-height: 100vh;
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle at 14% 6%, rgba(241, 135, 28, 0.1), transparent 28rem),
    radial-gradient(circle at 92% 24%, rgba(120, 183, 255, 0.08), transparent 24rem),
    var(--bg);
}

.login-shell,
.legal-shell {
  width: min(1240px, calc(100% - 36px));
  margin-inline: auto;
  padding: 28px 0 70px;
}

.login-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(340px, 0.8fr) minmax(0, 1.2fr);
  gap: 18px;
  align-items: start;
}

.login-panel {
  padding: 22px;
}

.login-primary-card {
  position: sticky;
  top: 22px;
}

.login-brand-row {
  display: flex;
  gap: 9px;
  align-items: center;
  margin-bottom: 28px;
  color: var(--faint);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.login-brand-row .brand-word {
  margin-right: 5px;
}

.auth-tabs {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 4px;
  margin: 20px 0 16px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  padding: 4px;
}

.auth-tab {
  min-height: 38px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 11px;
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.78rem;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.auth-tab.is-active {
  background: var(--surface);
  color: var(--text);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.auth-form {
  display: none;
  gap: 13px;
}

.auth-form.is-active {
  display: grid;
}

.login-side-stack {
  display: grid;
  gap: 18px;
}

.login-hero {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  gap: 24px;
  align-items: end;
  overflow: hidden;
  min-height: 310px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle at 90% 15%, rgba(241, 135, 28, 0.22), transparent 18rem),
    linear-gradient(145deg, var(--surface), var(--surface-muted));
  padding: 32px;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
}

.login-hero h1 {
  max-width: 760px;
  font-size: clamp(2rem, 4vw, 4rem);
}

.login-hero p:not(.eyebrow) {
  max-width: 660px;
}

.login-brand-symbol {
  display: grid;
  width: 92px;
  height: 92px;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 26px;
  background: var(--surface-glass);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-md);
}

.login-brand-symbol img {
  width: 70px;
  height: 70px;
}

.compact-login-info .role-action-grid {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
}

.created-panel,
.login-shell > .login-panel {
  margin-top: 18px;
}

.login-grid {
  grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0, 1fr));
}

.login-card {
  display: grid;
  min-width: 0;
  gap: 10px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  padding: 16px;
}

.login-card:hover {
  border-color: var(--line-strong);
}

.login-card .avatar-shell {
  width: 50px;
  height: 50px;
}

.login-card p {
  display: -webkit-box;
  margin: 0;
  overflow: hidden;
  font-size: 0.73rem;
  -webkit-box-orient: vertical;
  -webkit-line-clamp: 3;
}

.legal-top {
  position: sticky;
  top: 16px;
  z-index: 10;
  display: flex;
  gap: 20px;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: var(--surface-glass);
  padding: 10px 12px;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
  backdrop-filter: blur(22px);
}

.legal-top .brand-block {
  min-height: 44px;
}

.legal-tabs {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 5px;
  justify-content: flex-end;
}

.legal-tabs a,
.legal-tabs button {
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: transparent;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  font-weight: 650;
}

.legal-tabs .is-active {
  border-color: var(--line);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  color: var(--text);
}

.legal-document {
  max-width: 920px;
  margin-inline: auto;
  padding: clamp(24px, 5vw, 56px);
}

.legal-document > h1 {
  max-width: 780px;
  margin-bottom: 18px;
}

.legal-document > p {
  max-width: 760px;
  font-size: 0.96rem;
  line-height: 1.7;
}

.legal-section {
  margin-top: 30px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--line);
  padding-top: 24px;
}

.legal-section h2 {
  font-size: 1.18rem;
}

.legal-section p {
  margin-bottom: 0;
  line-height: 1.75;
}

.consent-brand {
  margin-bottom: 18px;
}

.consent-brand .brand-word {
  font-size: 1.25rem;
}

.consent-form {
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
  margin-top: 18px;
}

.consent-form .check-row {
  border: 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 0;
  background: transparent;
  padding: 12px 2px;
}

.consent-submit {
  min-height: 44px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent), var(--accent-2));
  color: var(--accent-ink);
  font-weight: 800;
}

.login-brand-glyph {
  color: var(--accent);
  font-size: 3rem;
  font-weight: 900;
  letter-spacing: -0.08em;
}

.consent-form .check-row:last-of-type { border-bottom: 0; }
.consent-form .check-row { align-items: flex-start; }
.consent-form .check-row input { margin-top: 3px; }
.consent-role-lead { margin-top: 12px; color: var(--muted); line-height: 1.55; }

/* Entrada de invitado: acceso independiente de las cuentas y perfiles de prueba. */
.guest-entry {
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
  margin: 20px 0 18px;
  padding: 16px;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
}

.guest-entry .eyebrow { margin: 0; }
.guest-entry small { color: var(--muted); line-height: 1.5; }
.guest-entry .primary-button { white-space: normal; min-height: 48px; }
```

**FIN DEL CONTENIDO: `auth-legal.css`**

<a id="archivo-css-base-css"></a>
### Archivo: `base.css`

**RUTA:** `css/base.css`

**INICIO DEL CONTENIDO: `base.css`**

```css
* {
  box-sizing: border-box;
}

html {
  min-width: 320px;
  background: var(--bg);
  color: var(--text);
  font-family:
    Inter,
    -apple-system,
    BlinkMacSystemFont,
    'Segoe UI',
    sans-serif;
  scroll-behavior: smooth;
}

body {
  margin: 0;
  min-width: 320px;
  min-height: 100vh;
  overflow-x: hidden;
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle at 72% -10%, rgba(241, 135, 28, 0.08), transparent 32rem), var(--bg);
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 15px;
  line-height: 1.5;
  transition:
    background-color var(--ease),
    color var(--ease);
}

button,
input,
select,
textarea {
  font: inherit;
}

button,
a {
  -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent;
}

button {
  color: inherit;
}

button:not(:disabled),
a[href] {
  cursor: pointer;
}

button:disabled {
  cursor: not-allowed;
  opacity: 0.5;
}

a {
  color: inherit;
  text-decoration: none;
}

img,
svg {
  display: block;
}

img {
  max-width: 100%;
}

svg {
  width: 1em;
  height: 1em;
}

h1,
h2,
h3,
p {
  margin-top: 0;
}

h1,
h2,
h3,
strong {
  color: var(--text-strong);
}

h1 {
  margin-bottom: 10px;
  font-size: clamp(2rem, 4vw, 4rem);
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: -0.055em;
  line-height: 0.98;
}

h2 {
  margin-bottom: 8px;
  font-size: clamp(1.25rem, 2vw, 1.8rem);
  font-weight: 750;
  letter-spacing: -0.035em;
  line-height: 1.08;
}

h3 {
  margin-bottom: 4px;
  font-size: 1rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: -0.015em;
}

p {
  margin-bottom: 14px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

::selection {
  background: rgba(241, 135, 28, 0.25);
}

:focus-visible {
  outline: 3px solid var(--focus);
  outline-offset: 2px;
}

input,
select,
textarea {
  width: 100%;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  color: var(--text);
  padding: 12px 14px;
  outline: none;
  transition:
    border-color var(--ease),
    box-shadow var(--ease),
    background-color var(--ease);
}

input:focus,
select:focus,
textarea:focus {
  border-color: var(--blue);
  box-shadow: 0 0 0 3px rgba(120, 183, 255, 0.18);
  background: var(--surface);
}

textarea {
  min-height: 120px;
  resize: vertical;
}

label {
  display: grid;
  gap: 7px;
  color: var(--text);
  font-size: 0.88rem;
  font-weight: 600;
}

.hidden,
[hidden] {
  display: none !important;
}

.visually-hidden {
  position: absolute !important;
  width: 1px !important;
  height: 1px !important;
  padding: 0 !important;
  margin: -1px !important;
  overflow: hidden !important;
  clip: rect(0, 0, 0, 0) !important;
  white-space: nowrap !important;
  border: 0 !important;
}

.eyebrow {
  margin: 0 0 8px;
  color: var(--accent);
  font-size: 0.7rem;
  font-weight: 800;
  letter-spacing: 0.11em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.support-copy {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.9rem;
  line-height: 1.6;
}

.full {
  width: 100%;
}

body.compact-ui .panel-card,
body.compact-ui .feed-card,
body.compact-ui .capsule-card {
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
}

body.high-contrast-ui {
  --line: rgba(23, 20, 20, 0.22);
  --line-strong: rgba(23, 20, 20, 0.36);
}

html[data-theme='dark'] body.high-contrast-ui {
  --line: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.22);
  --line-strong: rgba(255, 255, 255, 0.36);
}
```

**FIN DEL CONTENIDO: `base.css`**

<a id="archivo-css-components-css"></a>
### Archivo: `components.css`

**RUTA:** `css/components.css`

**INICIO DEL CONTENIDO: `components.css`**

```css
.primary-button,
.soft-button,
.danger-button,
.icon-button,
.top-icon,
.carousel-button,
.player-action,
.social-action,
.plain-link,
.switch-button {
  border: 0;
  transition:
    transform var(--ease),
    background-color var(--ease),
    border-color var(--ease),
    color var(--ease),
    box-shadow var(--ease),
    opacity var(--ease);
}

.primary-button,
.soft-button,
.danger-button {
  min-height: 42px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 10px 16px;
  font-size: 0.86rem;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.primary-button {
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent), var(--accent-2));
  color: var(--accent-ink);
  box-shadow: 0 9px 24px rgba(241, 135, 28, 0.2);
}

.primary-button:hover:not(:disabled) {
  transform: translateY(-1px);
  box-shadow: 0 12px 30px rgba(241, 135, 28, 0.28);
}

.soft-button {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: var(--surface-raised);
  color: var(--text);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.soft-button:hover:not(:disabled),
.soft-button.is-active {
  border-color: var(--line-strong);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.danger-button {
  border: 1px solid rgba(239, 101, 95, 0.28);
  background: rgba(239, 101, 95, 0.1);
  color: var(--error);
}

.compact-button {
  min-height: 34px;
  padding: 7px 11px;
  font-size: 0.75rem;
}

.icon-button,
.top-icon,
.carousel-button {
  display: inline-grid;
  place-items: center;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: var(--surface-raised);
  color: var(--muted);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.icon-button,
.top-icon {
  width: 40px;
  height: 40px;
  border-radius: 50%;
}

.icon-button.big {
  width: 48px;
  height: 48px;
}

.icon-button:hover,
.top-icon:hover,
.carousel-button:hover {
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  color: var(--text);
  transform: translateY(-1px);
}

.top-icon svg,
.icon-button svg {
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
}

.top-icon[data-open-notifications] {
  position: relative;
}

.top-icon[data-open-notifications]::after {
  position: absolute;
  top: 5px;
  right: 5px;
  display: none;
  width: 7px;
  height: 7px;
  border: 2px solid var(--surface);
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--error);
  content: '';
}

.top-icon[data-open-notifications][data-has-unread='true']::after {
  display: block;
}

.panel-card,
.feed-card,
.login-panel,
.legal-document,
.plan-card {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: var(--surface);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-sm);
}

.panel-card {
  padding: 22px;
}

.panel-head {
  display: flex;
  gap: 14px;
  align-items: flex-start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: 17px;
}

.panel-head h1,
.panel-head h2,
.panel-head h3,
.panel-head p {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

.small-head {
  align-items: center;
}

.counter-pill,
.status-chip,
.tag,
.role-pill-row > span,
.community-tags > span {
  display: inline-flex;
  min-height: 28px;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  padding: 5px 10px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.69rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.tag-row,
.role-pill-row,
.community-tags {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 6px;
}

.tag-row .tag:nth-child(4n + 1) {
  border-color: rgba(120, 183, 255, 0.24);
  background: rgba(120, 183, 255, 0.1);
}

.tag-row .tag:nth-child(4n + 2) {
  border-color: rgba(80, 201, 169, 0.24);
  background: rgba(80, 201, 169, 0.1);
}

.tag-row .tag:nth-child(4n + 3) {
  border-color: rgba(239, 124, 159, 0.24);
  background: rgba(239, 124, 159, 0.1);
}

.action-row,
.control-row,
.profile-actions-row,
.onboarding-actions,
.social-comment-actions {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 9px;
  align-items: center;
}

.stack-list,
.settings-list,
.insight-list,
.comment-list {
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
}

.list-item {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 48px minmax(0, 1fr) auto;
  gap: 12px;
  align-items: center;
  min-height: 64px;
  border: 1px solid transparent;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  padding: 8px 10px;
}

.list-item.clickable {
  width: 100%;
  color: inherit;
  text-align: left;
}

.list-item.clickable:hover {
  border-color: var(--line-strong);
  background: var(--surface-raised);
}

.list-cover {
  display: grid;
  width: 48px;
  height: 48px;
  place-items: center;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: 12px;
  background: var(--tone-soft, var(--surface));
}

.list-cover img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
}

.cover-symbol {
  display: grid;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  place-items: center;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--tone-a, var(--accent)), var(--tone-b, var(--accent-2)));
  color: #171414;
  font-size: 1.35rem;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.list-item-content {
  min-width: 0;
}

.list-item-content strong,
.list-item-content span {
  display: block;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.list-item-content strong {
  font-size: 0.85rem;
}

.list-item-content span {
  margin-top: 2px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
}

.mini-card,
.insight-card,
.role-feature,
.privacy-card,
.checklist-card,
.role-dossier,
.ai-card,
.invoice-card,
.playlist-card,
.community-mini {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  padding: 14px;
}

.mini-card strong,
.mini-card span,
.insight-card strong,
.insight-card span {
  display: block;
}

.mini-card span,
.insight-card span,
.role-feature span,
.privacy-card p,
.checklist-card p,
.role-dossier p {
  margin-top: 4px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.76rem;
}

.empty-state {
  border: 1px dashed var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  padding: 22px;
  color: var(--muted);
  text-align: center;
}

.plain-link {
  background: transparent;
  color: var(--accent);
  font-size: 0.75rem;
  font-weight: 700;
}

.file-picker {
  border: 1px dashed var(--line-strong);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  padding: 14px;
}

.file-picker input {
  display: none;
}

.file-picker strong {
  color: var(--accent);
}

.file-picker small {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-weight: 500;
}

.check-row {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 18px minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 10px;
  align-items: start;
  font-size: 0.78rem;
  font-weight: 500;
  line-height: 1.5;
}

.check-row input {
  width: 16px;
  height: 16px;
  margin-top: 2px;
  accent-color: var(--accent);
}

.legal-note {
  border-left: 3px solid var(--blue);
  border-radius: 0 var(--radius-sm) var(--radius-sm) 0;
  background: rgba(120, 183, 255, 0.08);
  padding: 11px 13px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.76rem;
}

.switch-button {
  position: relative;
  flex: 0 0 44px;
  width: 44px;
  height: 26px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px var(--line-strong);
}

.switch-button span {
  position: absolute;
  top: 4px;
  left: 4px;
  width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--faint);
  transition:
    transform var(--ease),
    background-color var(--ease);
}

.switch-button.is-on {
  background: rgba(70, 194, 121, 0.16);
  box-shadow: inset 0 0 0 1px rgba(70, 194, 121, 0.35);
}

.switch-button.is-on span {
  background: var(--success);
  transform: translateX(18px);
}

.toast {
  position: fixed;
  right: 22px;
  bottom: 22px;
  z-index: 100;
  max-width: min(360px, calc(100vw - 44px));
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--text-strong);
  padding: 12px 15px;
  color: var(--bg);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-lg);
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  opacity: 0;
  pointer-events: none;
  transform: translateY(12px);
  transition: 180ms ease;
}

.toast.is-visible {
  opacity: 1;
  transform: translateY(0);
}

.modal-layer,
.consent-overlay {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0;
  z-index: 80;
  display: grid;
  place-items: center;
  overflow-y: auto;
  background: rgba(8, 8, 10, 0.55);
  padding: 24px;
  backdrop-filter: blur(16px);
}

.modal-layer > article,
.modal-layer > section,
.modal-layer > div:not(.player-fullscreen-backdrop),
.consent-card {
  width: min(620px, 100%);
  max-height: min(84vh, 900px);
  overflow-y: auto;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-xl);
  background: var(--surface);
  padding: 24px;
  box-shadow: var(--shadow-lg);
}

.modal-layer h2,
.consent-card h2 {
  margin-bottom: 8px;
}

.modal-layer > article > p,
.modal-layer > section > p,
.modal-layer > div > p,
.consent-card > p {
  color: var(--muted);
}

/* Capas de interacción generadas por JavaScript */
[data-notification-badge] {
  position: absolute;
  top: -5px;
  right: -5px;
  display: grid;
  min-width: 18px;
  height: 18px;
  place-items: center;
  border: 2px solid var(--surface);
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--error);
  padding-inline: 4px;
  color: #fff;
  font-size: 0.58rem;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.ai-output,
.guide-hero,
.guide-role-note,
.onboarding-step,
.notification-card,
.chat-bubble {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
}

.ai-output {
  display: grid;
  gap: 4px;
  margin: 14px 0;
  padding: 14px;
}

.ai-output span,
.guide-role-note p,
.chat-bubble span,
.chat-bubble small {
  color: var(--muted);
}

.guide-hero,
.guide-role-note {
  margin-bottom: 14px;
  padding: 16px;
}

.guide-hero h2,
.guide-role-note p {
  margin-bottom: 0;
}

.onboarding-hero {
  margin-bottom: 16px;
}

.onboarding-steps,
.notification-modal-list,
.chat-thread {
  display: grid;
  gap: 9px;
}

.onboarding-step {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 30px minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 12px;
}

.onboarding-step > b {
  display: grid;
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: var(--accent-soft);
  color: var(--accent);
}

.notification-modal-head {
  display: flex;
  gap: 12px;
  align-items: start;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin-bottom: 14px;
}

.notification-card {
  display: grid;
  gap: 10px;
  padding: 13px;
}

.notification-card.is-community {
  border-color: color-mix(in srgb, var(--turquoise) 38%, var(--line));
}

.notification-card-main {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 34px minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 10px;
  align-items: start;
}

.notification-card-icon {
  display: grid;
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 11px;
  background: var(--accent-soft);
  color: var(--accent);
}

.notification-card-icon.is-community {
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--turquoise) 18%, transparent);
  color: var(--turquoise);
}

.notification-card-icon svg {
  width: 17px;
  height: 17px;
}

.notification-card-topline,
.notification-actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
}

.notification-card-copy {
  min-width: 0;
}

.notification-card-copy p {
  margin: 4px 0 0;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.76rem;
}

.notification-actions {
  justify-content: flex-end;
}

.friend-layout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1fr) minmax(260px, 0.8fr);
  gap: 14px;
  margin: 16px 0;
}

.friend-chat-form,
.comment-form {
  display: grid;
  gap: 12px;
}

.chat-thread {
  max-height: 260px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  margin: 12px 0 16px;
}

.chat-bubble {
  display: grid;
  gap: 3px;
  padding: 11px 12px;
}

.quick-comment-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 7px;
}

.quick-comment-grid .soft-button {
  justify-content: flex-start;
}

.social-comment-actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 8px;
  justify-content: flex-end;
}

.compact-ui .panel-card,
.compact-ui .capsule-card,
.compact-ui .feed-card {
  padding-top: 14px;
  padding-bottom: 14px;
}

.high-contrast-ui {
  --line: color-mix(in srgb, var(--text) 24%, transparent);
  --line-strong: color-mix(in srgb, var(--text) 42%, transparent);
  --muted: color-mix(in srgb, var(--text) 72%, transparent);
}

.private-mode [data-account-meta]::after {
  content: ' · Privado';
  color: var(--turquoise);
}

/* Notificaciones y consentimientos con menos ruido visual */
.notification-modal-list .notification-card {
  border: 0;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 0;
  background: transparent;
  padding: 12px 0;
}

.notification-modal-list .notification-card:last-child { border-bottom: 0; }
.notification-modal-list .notification-card.is-community { border-color: var(--line); }

.consent-heading {
  display: flex;
  gap: 14px;
  align-items: center;
}

.consent-legal-icon,
.modal-icon {
  display: grid;
  width: 44px;
  height: 44px;
  flex: 0 0 44px;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 14px;
  background: var(--accent-soft);
  color: var(--accent);
}

.consent-legal-icon svg, .modal-icon svg { width: 22px; height: 22px; }
.consent-heading .consent-brand { margin: 0 0 3px; }
.consent-heading h2 { margin: 0; }

.consent-legal-links {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 8px 14px;
  margin-top: 12px;
}

.consent-legal-links a {
  color: var(--accent);
  font-size: 0.76rem;
  font-weight: 700;
}

/* Los estados secundarios se leen como contenido, no como una cuadrícula de cápsulas. */
.role-workspace-section .privacy-card,
.role-workspace-section .mini-card {
  border-width: 0 0 1px;
  border-radius: 0;
  background: transparent;
  padding: 11px 0;
}

.role-workspace-section .privacy-card:last-child,
.role-workspace-section .mini-card:last-child {
  border-bottom: 0;
}

.notification-card-copy strong {
  font-size: 0.86rem;
  line-height: 1.25;
}

.notification-card-copy p {
  line-height: 1.45;
}

.consent-card {
  width: min(680px, 100%);
}
```

**FIN DEL CONTENIDO: `components.css`**

<a id="archivo-css-public-css"></a>
### Archivo: `public.css`

**RUTA:** `css/public.css`

**INICIO DEL CONTENIDO: `public.css`**

```css
:root {
  --accent: #f1871c;
  --accent-2: #ffb55e;
  --text: #171414;
  --muted: #65594d;
  --faint: #83776c;
  --bg: #ffffff;
  --surface: #ffffff;
  --soft: #fbf7f2;
  --line: rgba(23, 20, 20, 0.11);
  --blue: #78b7ff;
  --turquoise: #50c9a9;
  --green: #57c97e;
  --pink: #ef7c9f;
  --error: #ef655f;
  --success: #46c279;
  --shadow: 0 18px 50px rgba(23, 20, 20, 0.08);
  --radius-sm: 14px;
  --radius-md: 20px;
  --radius-lg: 30px;
  color-scheme: light dark;
  font-family: Inter, ui-sans-serif, system-ui, -apple-system, BlinkMacSystemFont, "Segoe UI", sans-serif;
}

@media (prefers-color-scheme: dark) {
  :root {
    --text: #f7f3ee;
    --muted: #b8aea3;
    --faint: #948b82;
    --bg: #0f0f12;
    --surface: #17171b;
    --soft: #181719;
    --line: rgba(247, 243, 238, 0.12);
    --shadow: 0 20px 58px rgba(0, 0, 0, 0.32);
  }
}

* { box-sizing: border-box; }
html { scroll-behavior: smooth; }
body {
  margin: 0;
  background:
    radial-gradient(circle at 88% 4%, rgba(80, 201, 169, .10), transparent 28rem),
    radial-gradient(circle at 10% 26%, rgba(120, 183, 255, .08), transparent 26rem),
    var(--bg);
  color: var(--text);
  line-height: 1.6;
}
img { max-width: 100%; }
a { color: inherit; }
button, a { -webkit-tap-highlight-color: transparent; }
button { font: inherit; }
:focus-visible { outline: 3px solid color-mix(in srgb, var(--blue) 70%, transparent); outline-offset: 3px; }

.skip-link {
  position: fixed;
  top: 8px;
  left: 8px;
  z-index: 100;
  transform: translateY(-160%);
  border-radius: 10px;
  background: var(--text);
  padding: 9px 12px;
  color: var(--bg);
  text-decoration: none;
}
.skip-link:focus { transform: translateY(0); }

.public-header {
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 30;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--bg) 84%, transparent);
  backdrop-filter: blur(22px) saturate(1.2);
}
.public-nav {
  display: flex;
  width: min(1180px, calc(100% - 36px));
  min-height: 72px;
  margin-inline: auto;
  gap: 24px;
  align-items: center;
}
.public-brand {
  display: inline-flex;
  gap: 9px;
  align-items: baseline;
  text-decoration: none;
  white-space: nowrap;
}
.public-brand strong { font-size: 1.35rem; letter-spacing: -0.06em; }
.public-brand small { color: var(--muted); font-size: .72rem; font-weight: 700; text-transform: uppercase; letter-spacing: .08em; }
.public-links { display: flex; gap: 6px; align-items: center; margin-left: auto; }
.public-links a {
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 8px 11px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: .82rem;
  font-weight: 650;
  text-decoration: none;
}
.public-links a:hover, .public-links a[aria-current="page"] { background: var(--soft); color: var(--text); }

.public-action, .public-secondary, .share-page {
  display: inline-flex;
  min-height: 42px;
  gap: 8px;
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: center;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: 999px;
  padding: 10px 16px;
  font-weight: 750;
  text-decoration: none;
  cursor: pointer;
}
.public-action { background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent), var(--accent-2)); color: #171414; box-shadow: 0 10px 26px rgba(241,135,28,.22); }
.public-secondary, .share-page { border: 1px solid var(--line); background: var(--surface); color: var(--text); }
.share-page svg { width: 17px; height: 17px; }

.public-main { width: min(1120px, calc(100% - 36px)); margin-inline: auto; }
.public-hero {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: minmax(0, 1.15fr) minmax(280px, .85fr);
  gap: clamp(28px, 6vw, 80px);
  align-items: center;
  padding: clamp(62px, 10vw, 118px) 0 52px;
}
.eyebrow { margin: 0 0 10px; color: var(--accent); font-size: .72rem; font-weight: 850; letter-spacing: .12em; text-transform: uppercase; }
h1, h2, h3 { margin: 0; line-height: 1.06; letter-spacing: -.045em; }
h1 { max-width: 840px; font-size: clamp(2.8rem, 7vw, 5.8rem); }
h2 { font-size: clamp(1.8rem, 4vw, 3rem); }
h3 { font-size: 1.08rem; letter-spacing: -.02em; }
.lead { max-width: 760px; margin: 22px 0 0; color: var(--muted); font-size: clamp(1.04rem, 1.7vw, 1.22rem); }
.hero-actions { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px; margin-top: 20px; }
.hero-art-card {
  position: relative;
  min-height: 340px;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 36px;
  background: linear-gradient(145deg, rgba(80,201,169,.27), rgba(120,183,255,.20) 48%, rgba(239,124,159,.16));
  box-shadow: var(--shadow);
}
.hero-art-card::before, .hero-art-card::after { position: absolute; border-radius: 50%; content: ''; }
.hero-art-card::before { width: 230px; height: 230px; top: -48px; left: -48px; background: rgba(255,255,255,.18); }
.hero-art-card::after { width: 150px; height: 150px; right: 38px; bottom: 30px; border: 36px solid rgba(23,20,20,.18); }
.hero-art-copy { position: absolute; inset: auto 30px 30px; z-index: 2; }
.hero-art-copy strong { display: block; font-size: 1.55rem; }
.hero-art-copy span { color: var(--muted); }

.quick-summary, .public-card, .faq, .legal-public-document, .team-card, .comparison-wrap {
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--surface) 94%, transparent);
  box-shadow: var(--shadow);
}
.quick-summary { display: grid; grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0,1fr); gap: 16px; margin: 8px 0 54px; padding: 20px 22px; }
.quick-summary-icon { display: grid; width: 42px; height: 42px; place-items: center; border-radius: 14px; background: rgba(80,201,169,.15); color: var(--turquoise); font-weight: 900; }
.quick-summary ul { margin: 8px 0 0; padding-left: 18px; color: var(--muted); }

.public-section { padding: 46px 0; }
.section-intro { display: grid; grid-template-columns: minmax(0,.7fr) minmax(280px,1.3fr); gap: 28px; align-items: start; margin-bottom: 24px; }
.section-intro p { margin: 0; color: var(--muted); }
.card-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(3, minmax(0,1fr)); gap: 14px; }
.public-card { padding: 22px; }
.public-card p { margin: 8px 0 0; color: var(--muted); }
.accent-blue { border-top: 3px solid var(--blue); }
.accent-green { border-top: 3px solid var(--green); }
.accent-pink { border-top: 3px solid var(--pink); }
.accent-turquoise { border-top: 3px solid var(--turquoise); }
.accent-orange { border-top: 3px solid var(--accent); }
.public-list { display: grid; gap: 0; margin: 0; padding: 0; list-style: none; }
.public-list li { position: relative; padding: 13px 0 13px 24px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); color: var(--muted); }
.public-list li:last-child { border-bottom: 0; }
.public-list li::before { position: absolute; top: 21px; left: 3px; width: 7px; height: 7px; border-radius: 50%; background: var(--success); content: ''; }

.comparison-wrap { overflow-x: auto; padding: 6px 20px 18px; }
table { width: 100%; border-collapse: collapse; min-width: 650px; }
th, td { padding: 14px 10px; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line); text-align: left; vertical-align: top; }
th { color: var(--text); font-size: .82rem; }
td { color: var(--muted); font-size: .83rem; }

.faq { margin-top: 24px; padding: 26px; }
.faq-list { display: grid; gap: 0; margin-top: 16px; }
.faq-item { padding: 17px 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }
.faq-item h3 { margin-bottom: 7px; }
.faq-item p { margin: 0; color: var(--muted); }

.team-grid { display: grid; grid-template-columns: repeat(4, minmax(0,1fr)); gap: 14px; }
.team-card { padding: 18px; }
.team-card img { display: block; width: 72px; height: 72px; border-radius: 50%; margin-bottom: 14px; }
.team-card p { margin: 6px 0 0; color: var(--muted); font-size: .86rem; }

.breadcrumb { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 7px; margin: 34px 0 0; color: var(--faint); font-size: .78rem; }
.breadcrumb a { color: var(--muted); text-decoration: none; }
.legal-public-document { margin: 24px 0 60px; padding: clamp(24px, 5vw, 54px); }
.legal-public-document > .lead { margin-bottom: 18px; }
.legal-section { padding: 24px 0; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }
.legal-section h2 { font-size: 1.45rem; }
.legal-section p { margin: 9px 0 0; color: var(--muted); }

.public-footer { margin-top: 70px; border-top: 1px solid var(--line); }
.public-footer-inner { display: grid; width: min(1120px, calc(100% - 36px)); margin-inline: auto; grid-template-columns: 1fr auto; gap: 24px; padding: 34px 0 48px; }
.public-footer p { margin: 5px 0 0; color: var(--muted); font-size: .8rem; }
.footer-links { display: flex; flex-wrap: wrap; gap: 10px 16px; justify-content: flex-end; }
.footer-links a { color: var(--muted); font-size: .78rem; text-decoration: none; }
.share-status { min-height: 1.4em; margin-top: 8px; color: var(--muted); font-size: .76rem; }
.mobile-sticky-cta { display: none; }

@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .public-hero, .section-intro { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .hero-art-card { min-height: 280px; }
  .card-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0,1fr)); }
  .team-grid { grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0,1fr)); }
  .public-links a:nth-child(n+4) { display: none; }
}

@media (max-width: 680px) {
  body { padding-bottom: calc(72px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom)); }
  .public-nav { min-height: 62px; }
  .public-brand small, .public-links { display: none; }
  .public-nav > .public-action { margin-left: auto; min-height: 38px; padding: 8px 12px; font-size: .78rem; }
  .public-hero { padding-top: 48px; }
  h1 { font-size: clamp(2.35rem, 13vw, 4rem); }
  .card-grid, .team-grid { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .quick-summary { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .public-footer-inner { grid-template-columns: 1fr; }
  .footer-links { justify-content: flex-start; }
  .mobile-sticky-cta {
    position: fixed;
    right: 12px;
    bottom: calc(10px + env(safe-area-inset-bottom));
    left: 12px;
    z-index: 50;
    display: flex;
    min-height: 50px;
    align-items: center;
    justify-content: center;
    border: 1px solid rgba(255,255,255,.22);
    border-radius: 999px;
    background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent), var(--accent-2));
    color: #171414;
    box-shadow: 0 16px 40px rgba(0,0,0,.20);
    font-weight: 850;
    text-decoration: none;
  }
}

.visually-hidden {
  position: absolute !important;
  width: 1px !important;
  height: 1px !important;
  overflow: hidden !important;
  clip: rect(0 0 0 0) !important;
  clip-path: inset(50%) !important;
  white-space: nowrap !important;
}
```

**FIN DEL CONTENIDO: `public.css`**

<a id="archivo-css-responsive-css"></a>
### Archivo: `responsive.css`

**RUTA:** `css/responsive.css`

**INICIO DEL CONTENIDO: `responsive.css`**

```css
@media (max-width: 1180px) {
  :root {
    --sidebar-width: 230px;
  }

  .content-area {
    padding-inline: 22px;
  }

  .plan-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .login-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }
}

@media (max-width: 1100px) {
  :root {
    --sidebar-width: 270px;
  }

  .sidebar {
    transform: translateX(-104%);
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-lg);
    transition: transform 220ms ease;
  }

  body.sidebar-open .sidebar {
    transform: translateX(0);
  }

  .sidebar-scrim {
    position: fixed;
    inset: 0;
    z-index: 35;
    display: block;
    border: 0;
    background: rgba(8, 8, 10, 0.45);
    opacity: 0;
    pointer-events: none;
    transition: opacity var(--ease);
  }

  body.sidebar-open .sidebar-scrim {
    opacity: 1;
    pointer-events: auto;
  }

  .workspace {
    margin-left: 0;
  }

  .topbar {
    padding-inline: 18px;
  }

  [data-mobile-menu],
  .mobile-brand {
    display: inline-grid;
  }

  .mobile-brand {
    margin-left: 8px;
  }

  .mobile-brand .brand-word {
    font-size: 1.2rem;
  }

  .topbar-actions {
    margin-left: auto;
  }

  .topbar-context {
    display: grid;
  }

  .interaction-layout {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .sticky-panel {
    position: static;
  }

  .now-playing-bar {
    width: min(620px, calc(100vw - 36px));
  }
}

@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .hero-panel,
  .player-panel,
  .player-fullscreen-body,
  .login-layout {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .hero-panel {
    min-height: 0;
  }

  .hero-art {
    width: min(100%, 330px);
    justify-self: start;
    order: -1;
    transform: none;
  }

  .player-panel .cover-art {
    width: min(100%, 420px);
  }

  .player-fullscreen-cover {
    width: min(100%, 420px);
  }

  .login-primary-card {
    position: static;
  }

  .role-action-grid,
  .profile-summary-grid,
  .role-dashboard-grid,
  .role-aware-metrics,
  .community-signal-grid,
  .phase-mini-grid,
  .artist-health-grid,
  .scouting-responsible-grid {
    grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  }

  .discover-summary-content {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 720px) {
  :root {
    --topbar-height: 64px;
  }

  body {
    padding-bottom: 72px;
  }

  .content-area {
    padding: 20px 14px 120px;
  }

  .topbar {
    height: var(--topbar-height);
    padding-inline: 12px;
  }

  .help-top-icon {
    display: none;
  }

  .top-icon,
  .icon-button {
    width: 38px;
    height: 38px;
  }

  .section-heading {
    display: grid;
    gap: 12px;
    margin: 26px 0 14px;
  }

  .section-actions {
    justify-content: flex-start;
  }

  .section-heading h1,
  .section-heading h2 {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }

  .hero-panel {
    gap: 22px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 18px;
  }

  .hero-copy h1,
  .hero-copy h2 {
    font-size: 2.35rem;
  }

  .hero-art {
    width: min(100%, 280px);
    border-radius: 22px;
  }

  .hero-copy .tag-row .tag:nth-child(n + 3) {
    display: none;
  }

  .hero-copy .action-row .soft-button:last-child {
    display: none;
  }

  .capsule-carousel {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .carousel-button {
    display: none;
  }

  .capsule-grid {
    margin-inline: -14px;
    padding-inline: 14px;
    scroll-snap-type: x proximity;
  }

  .capsule-card {
    --card-width: min(78vw, 272px);
    scroll-snap-align: start;
  }

  .two-column,
  .billing-grid,
  .publish-layout,
  .player-secondary-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .player-panel {
    gap: 22px;
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 18px;
  }

  .player-copy h1,
  .player-copy h2 {
    font-size: 2.3rem;
  }

  .player-quick-actions {
    justify-content: flex-start;
  }

  .player-mode-action {
    display: none;
  }

  .feed-body-head {
    display: block;
  }

  .feed-body-head .counter-pill {
    margin-top: 10px;
  }

  .community-tags > span:nth-child(n + 3),
  .social-stats-row {
    display: none;
  }

  .panel-card {
    padding: 17px;
  }

  .plan-grid,
  .login-grid,
  .role-action-grid,
  .profile-summary-grid,
  .role-dashboard-grid,
  .role-aware-metrics,
  .community-signal-grid,
  .phase-mini-grid,
  .artist-health-grid,
  .scouting-responsible-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .bottom-nav {
    position: fixed;
    right: 8px;
    bottom: 8px;
    left: 8px;
    z-index: 50;
    display: grid;
    grid-template-columns: repeat(5, minmax(0, 1fr));
    gap: 3px;
    border: 1px solid var(--line);
    border-radius: 20px;
    background: var(--surface-glass);
    padding: 6px;
    box-shadow: var(--shadow-lg);
    backdrop-filter: blur(24px);
  }

  .bottom-nav button {
    display: grid;
    min-height: 48px;
    place-items: center;
    gap: 2px;
    border: 0;
    border-radius: 14px;
    background: transparent;
    color: var(--faint);
    font-size: 0.59rem;
  }

  .bottom-nav button.is-active {
    background: rgba(241, 135, 28, 0.12);
    color: var(--accent);
  }

  .bottom-nav .nav-icon {
    width: 22px;
    height: 22px;
  }

  .bottom-nav .nav-icon svg {
    width: 17px;
    height: 17px;
  }

  .bottom-nav button[data-view-target='billing'] {
    display: none;
  }

  .now-playing-bar {
    right: 8px;
    bottom: 76px;
    width: calc(100vw - 16px);
    border-radius: 18px;
  }

  .mini-now-controls .icon-button:nth-of-type(1),
  .mini-now-controls .icon-button:nth-of-type(3),
  .mini-now-controls .icon-button:nth-of-type(4),
  .mini-now-controls .icon-button:nth-of-type(5) {
    display: none;
  }

  .modal-layer,
  .consent-overlay {
    align-items: end;
    padding: 10px;
  }

  .modal-layer > article,
  .modal-layer > section,
  .modal-layer > div:not(.player-fullscreen-backdrop),
  .consent-card {
    width: 100%;
    max-height: 88vh;
    border-radius: 26px;
    padding: 19px;
  }

  .theme-choice-grid {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .player-fullscreen-backdrop {
    align-items: end;
    padding: 8px;
  }

  .player-fullscreen-shell {
    max-height: 92vh;
    overflow-y: auto;
    border-radius: 26px;
  }

  .player-fullscreen-body {
    gap: 22px;
    padding: 14px 18px 24px;
  }

  .login-shell,
  .legal-shell {
    width: min(100% - 24px, 1240px);
    padding-top: 16px;
  }

  .login-hero {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
    min-height: 0;
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
    padding: 22px;
  }

  .login-brand-symbol {
    width: 72px;
    height: 72px;
  }

  .legal-top {
    position: static;
    display: grid;
    gap: 8px;
  }

  .legal-tabs {
    justify-content: flex-start;
    overflow-x: auto;
    flex-wrap: nowrap;
    padding-bottom: 2px;
  }

  .legal-document {
    border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  }
}

@media (max-width: 440px) {
  .topbar-actions .top-icon:nth-child(2) {
    display: none;
  }

  .hero-copy h1,
  .player-copy h1,
  .player-copy h2 {
    font-size: 2rem;
  }

  .capsule-card {
    --card-width: 82vw;
  }

  .profile-hero-mini,
  .role-profile-hero {
    grid-template-columns: auto minmax(0, 1fr);
  }

  .profile-hero-mini > :last-child,
  .role-profile-hero > :last-child {
    grid-column: 1 / -1;
  }
}

@media (max-width: 720px) {
  .topbar-context {
    display: none;
  }

  .friend-layout,
  .phase-panel-layout {
    grid-template-columns: 1fr;
  }

  .player-fullscreen-meta {
    gap: 12px;
  }

  .player-fullscreen-side-actions {
    gap: 5px;
  }

  .player-icon-button {
    width: 38px;
    height: 38px;
  }
}
@media (max-width: 900px) {
  .feed-card .feed-media {
    max-height: 270px;
  }

  .player-fullscreen-shell {
    --full-width: min(720px, calc(100vw - 32px));
    max-height: calc(100vh - 32px);
  }

  .player-fullscreen-cover {
    width: min(100%, 320px);
  }
}

@media (max-width: 560px) {
  .player-fullscreen-backdrop {
    padding: 8px;
  }

  .player-fullscreen-shell {
    --full-width: calc(100vw - 16px);
    max-height: calc(100vh - 16px);
    border-radius: 24px;
  }

  .player-fullscreen-body {
    padding-inline: 18px;
  }

  .feed-card .feed-media {
    max-height: 230px;
  }
}

@media (min-width: 1101px) {
  .topbar > [data-mobile-menu],
  .topbar > .mobile-brand {
    display: none;
  }
}
```

**FIN DEL CONTENIDO: `responsive.css`**

<a id="archivo-css-shell-css"></a>
### Archivo: `shell.css`

**RUTA:** `css/shell.css`

**INICIO DEL CONTENIDO: `shell.css`**

```css
.app-shell {
  min-height: 100vh;
}

.sidebar {
  position: fixed;
  inset: 0 auto 0 0;
  z-index: 40;
  display: flex;
  width: var(--sidebar-width);
  flex-direction: column;
  gap: 8px;
  overflow-y: auto;
  border-right: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: var(--surface-glass);
  padding: 22px 14px 18px;
  backdrop-filter: blur(26px) saturate(1.25);
  scrollbar-width: thin;
  scrollbar-color: var(--line-strong) transparent;
}

.sidebar::-webkit-scrollbar {
  width: 6px;
}

.sidebar::-webkit-scrollbar-thumb {
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--line-strong);
}

.brand-block {
  display: inline-flex;
  min-height: 54px;
  align-items: center;
  gap: 9px;
  align-self: flex-start;
  padding: 8px 12px;
  border-radius: var(--radius-md);
}

.brand-word {
  color: var(--text-strong);
  font-size: 1.55rem;
  font-weight: 850;
  letter-spacing: -0.07em;
  line-height: 1;
}

.brand-subtitle {
  color: var(--faint);
  font-size: 0.68rem;
  font-weight: 700;
  letter-spacing: 0.08em;
  text-transform: uppercase;
}

.brand-logo-pair,
.brand-symbol-pair {
  position: relative;
  display: inline-grid;
}

.brand-logo-pair img,
.brand-symbol-pair img {
  grid-area: 1 / 1;
}

.brand-logo-on-dark {
  opacity: 0;
}

html[data-theme='dark'] .brand-logo-on-light {
  opacity: 0;
}

html[data-theme='dark'] .brand-logo-on-dark {
  opacity: 1;
}

.profile-pill {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 44px minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 11px;
  align-items: center;
  width: 100%;
  margin: 8px 0 4px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: var(--radius-lg);
  background: transparent;
  padding: 9px 10px;
  text-align: left;
  transition:
    background-color var(--ease),
    transform var(--ease);
}

.profile-pill:hover {
  background: var(--surface-muted);
}

.avatar-shell {
  position: relative;
  display: grid;
  width: 44px;
  height: 44px;
  place-items: center;
  overflow: hidden;
  border: 1px solid var(--line);
  border-radius: 50%;
  background: linear-gradient(135deg, var(--accent), var(--turquoise));
  color: #171414;
  font-weight: 800;
}

.avatar-shell img {
  position: absolute;
  inset: 0;
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
}

.avatar-shell img.is-hidden {
  display: none;
}

.profile-pill strong,
.profile-pill small {
  display: block;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.profile-pill strong {
  font-size: 0.92rem;
}

.profile-pill small {
  margin-top: 2px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
}

.nav-list {
  display: grid;
  gap: 3px;
  margin-top: 8px;
}

.nav-item,
.tool-link,
.session-logout {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 30px minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 7px;
  align-items: center;
  width: 100%;
  min-height: 46px;
  border: 0;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: transparent;
  padding: 8px 11px;
  color: var(--muted);
  text-align: left;
  transition:
    background-color var(--ease),
    color var(--ease),
    transform var(--ease);
}

.nav-item:hover,
.tool-link:hover,
.session-logout:hover {
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  color: var(--text);
}

.nav-item.is-active {
  background: rgba(241, 135, 28, 0.11);
  color: var(--accent);
  font-weight: 700;
}

.nav-item.is-active::before {
  position: absolute;
  width: 3px;
  height: 22px;
  margin-left: -11px;
  border-radius: 999px;
  background: var(--accent);
  content: '';
}

.nav-item {
  position: relative;
}

.nav-icon,
.tool-icon {
  display: grid;
  width: 30px;
  height: 30px;
  place-items: center;
  border-radius: 9px;
}

.nav-icon svg,
.tool-icon svg {
  width: 17px;
  height: 17px;
}

.sidebar-card {
  margin-top: 10px;
  border-top: 1px solid var(--line);
  padding: 15px 7px 0;
}

.sidebar-card .eyebrow {
  margin-bottom: 7px;
  color: var(--faint);
  font-size: 0.61rem;
}

.session-card > strong,
.session-card > span {
  display: block;
  padding-inline: 4px;
}

.session-card > strong {
  font-size: 0.84rem;
}

.session-card > span {
  margin-top: 2px;
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.72rem;
  line-height: 1.35;
}

.session-grid {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: repeat(2, minmax(0, 1fr));
  gap: 7px;
  margin: 11px 0 8px;
}

.session-grid > span {
  display: grid;
  gap: 1px;
  min-height: 54px;
  align-content: center;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  padding: 8px 10px;
}

.session-grid b {
  font-size: 1rem;
}

.session-grid small {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.64rem;
}

.side-current {
  display: grid;
  grid-template-columns: 34px minmax(0, 1fr);
  gap: 9px;
  align-items: center;
  margin: 7px 0;
  border-radius: var(--radius-sm);
  background: var(--surface-muted);
  padding: 7px 9px;
}

.side-current b,
.side-current small {
  display: block;
  overflow: hidden;
  text-overflow: ellipsis;
  white-space: nowrap;
}

.side-current b {
  font-size: 0.76rem;
}

.side-current small {
  color: var(--muted);
  font-size: 0.65rem;
}

.side-dot {
  display: grid;
  width: 34px;
  height: 34px;
  place-items: center;
  overflow: hidden;
  border-radius: 9px;
  background: var(--tone-soft, var(--surface));
}

.side-dot img {
  width: 100%;
  height: 100%;
  object-fit: cover;
}

.session-logout {
  margin-top: 3px;
  color: var(--muted);
}

.session-logout svg {
  width: 17px;
  height: 17px;
}

.quick-tools {
  margin-top: auto;
}

.legal-links {
  display: grid;
  gap: 2px;
}

.legal-links a {
  border-radius: 10px;
  padding: 6px 5px;
  color: var(--faint);
  font-size: 0.68rem;
}

.legal-links a:hover {
  color: var(--text);
}

.workspace {
  min-height: 100vh;
  margin-left: var(--sidebar-width);
}

.topbar {
  position: sticky;
  top: 0;
  z-index: 30;
  display: flex;
  height: var(--topbar-height);
  align-items: center;
  justify-content: space-between;
  border-bottom: 1px solid var(--line);
  background: var(--surface-glass);
  padding: 0 max(24px, calc((100vw - var(--sidebar-width) - var(--content-max)) / 2));
  backdrop-filter: blur(26px) saturate(1.25);
}

.topbar-actions {
  display: flex;
  gap: 7px;
  align-items: center;
  margin-left: auto;
}

.mobile-brand,
[data-mobile-menu] {
  display: none;
}

.content-area {
  width: min(100%, var(--content-max));
  margin-inline: auto;
  padding: 32px 30px 120px;
}

.view {
  display: none;
  animation: view-in 240ms ease both;
}

.view.is-active {
  display: block;
}

@keyframes view-in {
  from {
    opacity: 0;
    transform: translateY(5px);
  }
  to {
    opacity: 1;
    transform: translateY(0);
  }
}

.section-heading {
  display: flex;
  gap: 24px;
  align-items: end;
  justify-content: space-between;
  margin: 36px 0 18px;
}

.section-heading h1,
.section-heading h2 {
  max-width: 820px;
  margin: 0;
  font-size: clamp(1.9rem, 3.4vw, 3.1rem);
}

.section-actions {
  display: flex;
  flex-wrap: wrap;
  gap: 8px;
  justify-content: flex-end;
}

.sidebar-scrim {
  display: none;
}

.bottom-nav {
  display: none;
}

.topbar-context {
  display: none;
  gap: 1px;
  margin-left: 4px;
}

.topbar-context strong {
  font-size: 0.82rem;
  letter-spacing: -0.01em;
}

.topbar-context small {
  color: var(--faint);
  font-size: 0.65rem;
}
```

**FIN DEL CONTENIDO: `shell.css`**
